#### 📝 Prompt

```
A young girl is taking a close-up selfie in her bedroom. The composition is a close-up of her chest and above, with her face dominating the frame. She&#39;s slightly looking down at the camera, her shoulders relaxed, her body slightly turned to the side, her eyes looking directly at the lens. Her expression is innocent and serene, her eyes clear and bright, radiating the unique curiosity and purity of a child. Her lips are slightly relaxed, with a gentle, natural smile playing at the corners of her mouth. The little girl has braided her soft, long black hair into two loose braids, one on each side. The braids begin behind her ears, with a moderate tightness, creating a natural, airy feel. The braids fall in front of her shoulders, secured with small, light pink elastic bands. A few stray hairs peek out from the braids, and soft baby hairs gently brush against her cheeks, clearly showing the realistic texture and flow of each strand. The overall braids are casual and natural, possessing a childlike, slightly messy charm, rather than an overly neat and tightly braided style. The face is round and full, with noticeable baby fat on the cheeks, abundant collagen, and a soft, undeveloped jawline, showcasing the round face contours characteristic of a child. The features are delicate and youthful, with large, bright, round eyes that are slightly wide-set, long, slightly curled natural eyelashes, and no eyeliner. The eyebrows are a light gray-brown with a natural, downy texture. The nose is small and slightly flat with a cute, rounded tip. The lips are full and soft, with a natural light pink color and delicate, naturally glossy skin. The face is completely makeup-free, maintaining the pure state of a child&#39;s natural skin. The skin retains the texture of real human skin, possessing the delicate smoothness and softness unique to infant skin. Fine pores, skin texture, highlight on the nose tip, a faint natural blush on the cheeks, and natural skin tone variations are visible. The skin surface has the soft, downy texture characteristic of infants, with very faint freckles on the cheeks and bridge of the nose, preserving realistic details and presenting a high-resolution, lifelike photographic quality. The ears are unadorned, small and delicate, with rounded, full earlobes and smooth, translucent skin. The wearer wears a soft, off-white cotton short-sleeved T-shirt with a round neck that fits naturally around the neck. The fabric is soft and skin-friendly, showing slight natural wrinkles from daily wear, and the garment is loose and comfortable. The scene is a cozy family bedroom with a warm beige wall as the background. To the left, a half-open, light-colored wooden door and a shadowy area are visible. To the right rear, a warm yellow bedside lamp is on. The background is naturally blurred, yet the interior space is still discernible, with soft, light-colored bedding faintly visible at the corner of the bed. The room is illuminated by a mixture of natural light and warm-toned artificial light. The figure is bathed in soft natural light, with even and soft lighting on the face. The right side of the background is illuminated by warm yellow light, while the left side is slightly darker, creating a very subtle yet realistic gradation of light and shadow. The eyes reflect natural window light, with soft, bright highlights and clear, window-shaped light spots reflected in the pupils, giving the eyes a clear and expressive look. This is a realistic selfie style taken with a front-facing phone camera. It features close-up shots, a slight wide-angle perspective, and realistic lens distance. The subject doesn&#39;t appear posed; it&#39;s like a casual, innocent everyday selfie taken by a little girl in her bedroom. The image is photorealistic, with ultra-realistic skin texture, visible pores, individual hair strands, natural facial details, soft indoor lighting, warm ambient light, shallow depth of field, high dynamic range, natural color grading, subtle sensor texture, and highly detailed 4K portrait photography. It captures the innocent expression of a young child, chubby cheeks, baby skin texture, natural lighting, a home environment, and the candid everyday moment. It also features double braids, pigtail braids, and loose, flyaway hairs.
```

```
I. Generating Objectives: Generate a complete, continuous 15-second Chinese fantasy martial arts short film. Overall Style Requirements: Cinematic realism, pure ancient Chinese fantasy aesthetics, mature and advanced martial arts action design, Arri Alexa cinematic camera, clear and stable facial micro-details, natural volumetric lighting, delicate film grain, 24fps cinematic motion texture, natural motion blur. II. Core Action Concepts: Cinematize and fantasyize the body principles in real martial arts: Agile movements, stable footwork, oblique dodges, strict distance control, feigned retreats to lure the enemy, seizing the attack line, quickly completing offensive and defensive reversals. Visual satisfaction comes from the following: Clear and readable spatial geometry, dangerous time differences, weapon distance differences, silk clothing movement, metal collisions, a finishing move with strong memorability. Avoid relying on the following methods to create &quot;coolness&quot;: Excessive meaningless sword energy, excessive light pollution, teleportation, random special effects, and illogical energy attacks. III. References and Environment Settings: Character Identity Anchor Points: @Image 1 → Character ID A｜Sword Immortal Senior Sister @Image 2 → Character ID B｜Junior Sister Environment DNA: All newly uploaded background and location reference images collectively determine the same set of environment DNA. Before formal composition, silently analyze and integrate the following compatible elements: realistic terrain, architectural language, spatial scale, materials, era, vegetation, water, weather, mountain fog, cloud movement, main light direction, reflection relationship, atmospheric depth, foreground/middle ground/background layers, character movement lines, and reasonable combat routes. Then, recombine them into a new space that is unique, unified, believable, physically reasonable, and spatially continuous. IV. Environmental Movement Principles: The background must always be vibrant, but absolutely neutral in narrative. Continuous natural movement includes: feng shui, mountain mist, vegetation, clothing, distant views, figures, dust, and reflections. Requirements: All movement must follow real physical laws, maintain natural parallax, and the environment must not create its own crisis. The environment must not help either side, and the environment must not change the outcome of the battle. Only when a character actually steps on, skims over, collides with, or moves the environment will the local environment be allowed to exhibit normal physical feedback, such as: dust rising, water ripples spreading, clothing hem shifting, and debris moving. V. Character Setting Character ID A｜Sword Immortal Senior Sister｜@Image 1 Always the same 25-30 year old East Asian woman: Oval face, fair and natural skin tone, dark almond eyes, long black hair, half-up, white jade hairpin, fixed figure, tall and slender. Always wears the same white embroidered silk Hanfu from white cloth boots to semi-transparent layered wide sleeves, with a consistent silver waist belt and jade pendant. Holds a single silver straight sword. Performance and Action Requirements: Extremely stable and calm, restrained movements, precise and effective reactions, and no unnecessary flashy actions. Character ID B｜Junior Sister｜@Image 2 Always the same 20-25 year old East Asian woman: With a round, lively face, black braided hair, and a petite figure, she always wore the same light green linen Hanfu, from black cloth shoes to wooden hairpins, with a dark belt and a single dark steel sword. In this section, she and her older master stood several meters away, observing the entire battle. A supporting character, an enemy spearman, wore a dark charcoal gray ancient-style martial arts outfit, wielding a long spear with a dark red tassel, and confronted the same white-clad swordsman. His movements were professional and explosive, truly demonstrating the advantage of long-range weaponry. The older master observed from a distance without interfering in the battle, only offering a judgment at the end. VI. Shot Structure 0–5s | Wide shot or long shot | Establishing the battle space and distance advantage. Camera approximately 24mm. Environment established. Shot slowly pans horizontally. Content: First, clearly establish the accurate positioning of both sides, the advantage of long-range weaponry, and the complete battle space. The same white-clad swordsman confronted the enemy spearman. The same junior sister and her older master stood several meters away, observing the battle. The enemy spearman suddenly erupted without warning, thrusting his spear at high speed along the center line of his body. The same white-clad swordswoman makes only a very narrow, single diagonal step, simultaneously: turning her waist to avoid the spearhead, using the side of the same silver straight sword to gently deflect the spearhead, never directly colliding with it. After delivering the force, she immediately returns to stillness, without any unnecessary movement. This shot clearly establishes the sense of long-weapon dominance. The first move demonstrates the senior sister&#39;s judgment and distance control, avoiding the spear, deflecting it, and returning to position—all executed seamlessly. 5–10 seconds | Medium shot or cowboy shot | Three-stage attack and &quot;disarmed&quot; reversal camera, approximately 40mm, stable tracking shot. The content maintains the same swordswoman in a white embroidered silk Hanfu, the same enemy spearman, the same spear, the same bystanders, and the same geographical space. The enemy completes a clearly readable three-stage continuous attack: a straight thrust to the chest, followed by a downward sweep of the spear shaft to the lower body, immediately striking the same white-clad swordswoman with the spear&#39;s butt. The enemy then precisely retreats half a step, deliberately leaving a seemingly open opening, and immediately, judging the opportunity, completely presses their body weight into a final, powerful straight thrust. In that instant, she didn&#39;t block directly, but instead: She again darted away from the spear&#39;s center line, using her silver sword to circle around the wooden shaft, guiding the spear&#39;s attack in a small circular motion. Then, she instantly rushed into the close-quarters area where the spear&#39;s range advantage was most difficult to exploit. The enemy reacted equally professionally, immediately spinning the spear shaft and using powerful lateral force to violently knock the same silver sword from her hand into the air. The camera lingered briefly on the spinning, rising silver sword, creating a clear visual reversal: &quot;The sword immortal seems to have been disarmed.&quot; At this moment: The same junior sister&#39;s eyes suddenly widen; the same older master remains motionless. This shot emphasizes the continuous pressure of the spear, clearly showing the sword immortal&#39;s strategy of luring the enemy. The knocked-away sword should be a memorable element. The junior sister shows emotional reaction, while the master remains stable. 10-15 seconds | Close-up or extremely close-up | Close-up blind spot, seizing the opportunity to finish the attack. Camera approx. 75mm. Emotional close-up. Maintain continuity in content: the same sword immortal, the same enemy, the same clothing, and all weapons. The enemy seizes the moment she loses her weapon and immediately thrusts his spear for a fatal strike. The same swordswoman didn&#39;t retreat at all. Instead, she proactively stepped into the enemy&#39;s blind spot, her movements clear and predictable: using her left forearm to control the safe area of ​​the wooden spear shaft, ensuring she didn&#39;t touch the blade; using her scabbard to block the spear shaft and continue forward; then, she spun past the enemy along his shoulder line, causing his forward momentum to disorient her. Simultaneously, the same silver straight sword that had been thrown into the air fell behind her. She didn&#39;t turn around, only extending her right hand naturally behind her to precisely catch the falling hilt, smoothly rotating her wrist to return to a normal grip. Just as the enemy turned, she stopped the sword&#39;s edge a finger&#39;s width from his throat. She couldn&#39;t actually pierce it. The camera zooms in with an extremely close-up, first showing the enemy&#39;s suddenly contracted pupils, then shifting the focus to the same white-clad swordswoman&#39;s calm eyes and a wisp of black hair gently swaying in the wind. With the outcome decided, no one was allowed to launch another attack. The same older master calmly said from a distance, &quot;You may have obtained the sword, but you cannot take away her power.&quot; The same junior sister was completely stunned. The final shot focuses on: the same white-clad swordsman slowly lowering his silver sword, while the enemy&#39;s spear tassel gradually stops swaying. This shot is crucial; the finishing action must be the most memorable moment of the entire film. The key is not the assassination, but the &quot;seizure of power.&quot; The three steps—close-range blind spot, catching the sword, and stopping the sword—must be extremely clear. VII. Sound and Movement Requirements: Sound: Native, synchronized; Mandarin dialogue: Precise; Spatial: Metal clashing sounds, spear cutting through the wind, footsteps, clothing fluttering, realistic breathing; Music must never drown out weapon sounds; Movement: Weapon trajectories must be clear; Character weight and inertia must be realistic, believable, and natural; Blurred vision, continuous weapon movement, stable continuity; No teleportation; No random sword energy; No subtitles. VIII. Strict Overall Output Requirements: Total duration: 15 seconds; Aspect Ratio: 16:9, landscape; Three consecutive clear shots; Optimized for Seedance 2.0 Fast; Strictly maintain consistency in character identities across multiple reference points; Stable geographic space; Always vivid background, but narratively neutral. IX. Negative blurry, bad quality, low quality, low resolution, noisy, jpeg artifacts, watermark, text, subtitles, error; deformed, mutated, bad anatomy, poorly drawn hands, bad composition, out of frame, disfigured; inconsistent character, changing clothes, face morphing, hairstyle change, background shift, glitching cuts, disappearing props, random sword aura, excessive energy effects, overexposed light pollution, teleporting movement, unreadable weapon trajectories, fake combat, weak body mechanics, exaggerated anime action, modern elements
```

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Generate a 30-second, 16:9 landscape-style, highly realistic live-action water obstacle course competition program. The overall feel is reminiscent of a live broadcast of a large-scale Japanese sports and entertainment special from the late 2000s to the 2010s. The stage is a massive outdoor water arena at night, with the entire course built above a large competition pool, surrounded by large lighting towers, steel trusses, spectator seating, staff areas, and real television program filming equipment. Multi-camera shooting is employed, including ENG cameras, crane cameras, track shooting, telephoto lenses, low-angle shots on the water, and fixed shots on the sides of obstacles. The camera consistently focuses on the participants&#39; actions. The footage maintains a realistic television broadcast feel: natural panning, zooming, focus tracking, participant tracking, a slight ENG camera movement, the slightly softer image quality of early high-definition television, natural television compression, and realistic light and shadow contrast created by large-scale stage lighting. The entire program follows natural, realistic speed and real-time physical laws. [Forced Participant Consistency] The uploaded image {{Mixed 1}} is the challenger&#39;s only visual reference. The video maintains strict consistency in the following aspects: the character&#39;s identity, facial features, face shape, hairstyle, hair color, skin tone, age, height, body shape, body proportions, clothing, and overall demeanor remain the same from the first frame to the last. This consistency is maintained across multiple camera angles, including front, side, back, low-angle, telephoto, full-body movements, running, jumping, falling, climbing, water-related activities, and wet conditions. The clothing remains the same as in the uploaded character images, with consistent color, structure, material, and wearing style. [Site Continuity] The entire video takes place in a single, continuous nighttime water arena. The three obstacles are connected in physical order: Roller Obstacle → Fishbone Reverse Obstacle → Recurve Wall. The character must continuously advance along the same track. Each camera cut continues the character&#39;s position and progress from the previous shot. The positional relationships of obstacles, pools, platforms, spectator seating, and large lights remain continuous and stable. [30-Second Timeline] 0–3.2 seconds | Challenger&#39;s Entrance [Legs Close-up → Upper Body Medium Shot → Face Close-up] The video begins directly with a close-up of the challenger&#39;s legs. The camera naturally moves upwards along the person&#39;s body: legs → waist and upper body → face close-up. The person stands in the center of the starting platform. The background shows the brightly lit large water obstacle course, steel structure facilities, and a large audience. The person appears relaxed and confident. When the camera focuses on her face, she naturally looks at the camera with a bright smile. Then, she gently clenches one fist and makes a simple, confident challenge gesture in front of her chest. The audience immediately applauds and cheers. The person then turns her head to look at the track ahead. The smile gradually fades. Her eyes become serious. She leans forward slightly, preparing to sprint. A brief, tense pause. 3.2–9 seconds | First Challenge: Large Roller Obstacle [3.2–4.3 seconds | Large Panoramic View] The starting buzzer suddenly sounds. The person immediately sprints out from the starting line at high speed. A large crane shot quickly reveals the entire arena and the first obstacle. Multiple large, flexible cylindrical rollers are arranged in a row on the water ahead. The audience erupts in cheers. [4.3–6.5 seconds | Diagonal Front ENG Follow Shot] The person approaches the rollers at high speed. She steps onto the first large roller. Upon contact, the roller immediately begins to rotate due to the weight of her body. The person&#39;s body is slightly pulled backward by the rotational inertia. She immediately spreads her arms wide to maintain balance. She then moves forward with quick, continuous small steps. With each step, the roller rotates accordingly. The person begins to show slight signs of disarray. [6.5–8 seconds | Side Track Follow Shot] A track camera moves at high speed parallel to the person. The person leaps over several rotating rollers in succession. At one point, her right foot clearly slips. Her body instantly leans to the side and back. She swings her arms quickly, twists her waist, and immediately regains her footing on the roller. The person does not fall. After regaining her balance, she immediately continues her sprint. [8–9 seconds | Backwards Telephoto] The character leaps forward forcefully from the last roller. Both feet land on a fixed platform. Knees bend naturally upon landing, and the body noticeably sinks to cushion the impact. The character immediately gets up and continues running forward. First obstacle successfully completed. 9–20 seconds | Second Obstacle: A massive water-based Fishbone Reverse reverse fishbone obstacle appears in front of the Fishbone Reverse. Multiple long, thin, flexible horizontal bars continuously rotate around a central mechanical axis. The character must move forward along a narrow, continuous platform while dodging the constantly sweeping rotating bars. This obstacle begins to make things noticeably more difficult for the character. [9–11 seconds | Crane Panoramic View] The character runs to the front of the Fishbone Reverse. The camera shows the entire area from above: the pool, the narrow platform, the central rotating structure, and the continuously rotating large flexible bars. The character observes for only a very short time before immediately entering the obstacle. [11–14 seconds | Diagonal Forward ENG / Track Follow Shot] The first rotating bar sweeps towards the character. The person quickly ducks and lowers their upper body, letting the bar pass overhead. Immediately, a second bar comes from the other direction. The person immediately sidesteps and takes a step forward. A third bar sweeps close to the legs. The person quickly lifts their leg and avoids it with a small jump. The entire movement continues forward without stopping to wait for each bar. The action begins to show obvious improvisation and a sense of disarray. [14–16 seconds | Low camera angle on the water] The camera is almost touching the water&#39;s surface. A large, rotating soft bar sweeps across the foreground at high speed. The person is moving quickly on a narrow platform behind them. A soft bar suddenly sweeps from the person&#39;s side and rear. She doesn&#39;t have time to completely avoid it. The bar makes a clear, realistic soft impact with the side of the person&#39;s body. The impact force immediately changes the person&#39;s center of gravity. The person&#39;s upper body is pulled to the side. They stumble two steps. Their arms instinctively swing wildly in an attempt to regain balance. [16–18 seconds | Side view ENG medium shot] The person ultimately doesn&#39;t fall into the water. However, they have completely lost their balance. She clearly sat down/half-fell onto the narrow platform. Her hips and one hand made actual contact with the platform. Her legs slid forward briefly. This was a genuine fall. There was a very brief, awkward pause after landing. Panic, surprise, and a hint of embarrassment appeared on her face. The audience erupted: gasps → laughter → cheers. [18–20 seconds | Follow shot from behind] She immediately used her hands to brace herself against the platform and stood up again. The movement was noticeably more rapid than at the beginning. Her hair and body swayed naturally with the movement. As she regained her balance, she continued forward. She ducked to avoid the last rotating bar. Then she quickly jumped onto the exit platform. She successfully left Fishbone Reverse. She didn&#39;t stop to rest. Despite her obvious fatigue, she rushed straight towards the final obstacle. 20–27 seconds | Final Obstacle: Recurve Wall The largest final obstacle in the entire track appeared in front of her: the giant recurve wall. The wall formed a distinct arc from the ground upwards, gradually becoming steeper towards the top. The top of the wall was significantly higher than her. At this point, she had already overcome two obstacles. Breathing became noticeably more rapid. The movement remained at full power. The high wall was ultimately attempted only once. [20–22 seconds | Frontal Telephoto] The person sprints at full speed towards the curved wall in a straight line. The telephoto lens compresses the space, making the massive wall appear directly in front of the person, occupying most of the frame. The person&#39;s arms swing noticeably during the run. The stride length gradually increases. The audience all begins to stand up. The cheers from the crowd rapidly rise. [22–24.5 seconds | Low-angle side view of the wall] The person maintains full speed as they sprint into the curved wall. The first foot lands heavily on the bottom of the wall. Then, relying on the momentum of running, they quickly take several steps upwards along the wall. The body gradually extends forward. At the highest point: the person simultaneously extends both hands upwards. Both hands successfully grab the edge of the top of the wall. The body leaves the wall. The legs dangle in the air. The arms bear the entire weight of the body. [24.5–27 seconds | Side view of the top of the wall (ENG + telephoto close-up)] The person firmly grips the top of the wall with both hands. They hang briefly. Then, they bend their arms and forcefully pull their upper body towards the top of the wall. The chest gradually approaches the edge of the wall. One hand readjusts its grip. One leg begins to lift. The scene must create a clear expectation in the viewer: &quot;She&#39;s got it, she&#39;s about to successfully flip over.&quot; Simultaneously, fatigue, strain, tension, and excitement at the prospect of success appear on the character&#39;s face. The audience&#39;s cheers reach their peak. 27–30 seconds | Sudden Trap → Fall from Height → Expression Ending [27–28 seconds | Medium Shot from the Side of the Wall] Just as the character is about to successfully flip over the wall—a hidden obstacle on the side of the wall suddenly activates. A huge, soft, multi-purpose mechanical hammer swings rapidly horizontally from the side. The hammer has a clear mechanical trajectory. The character has no time to dodge. The hammer strikes the character&#39;s shoulder and upper body side. After the impact, the character&#39;s body immediately produces a corresponding physical response: the shoulder is pushed to the other side. The upper body twists instantly. The grip is broken. One hand slips first. The other hand briefly catches the edge of the wall. Then it also loses its grip. A clear look of sudden surprise appears on the character&#39;s face. [28–29 seconds | Low-angle/Telephoto Falling Shot] The person is completely detached from the wall. Falling from a height at the speed of real gravity, the body naturally leans backward in the air. Arms instinctively spread out. Legs swing naturally. The camera keeps the person&#39;s entire body visible simultaneously, along with the pool below. The body continues downward, crashing directly into the water. A huge, realistic splash explodes instantly. Water ripples spread outward from the impact point. The audience erupts in gasps and laughter. [29–30 seconds | Close-up of the Person&#39;s Face on the Water Surface] The splash subsides. The person immediately resurfaces, their head and shoulders exposed. Hair is completely soaked, naturally clinging to the sides of the face and shoulders. The person breathes rapidly, initially appearing visibly dazed. Then, looking at the camera, their face displays an exaggerated expression of: grievance, helplessness, and a slight disbelief at their failure. The close-up of the person&#39;s expression is the final shot of the video. The video ends amidst cheers, laughter, and applause from the audience. [Physical Feedback] All obstacles must have a real, real-time physical relationship with the character&#39;s body. Roller: Stepping down → Roller rotates under force → Character&#39;s center of gravity changes. Fishbone: Rotating lever moves → Approaches the character → Dodges or makes real soft contact → Body&#39;s center of gravity is affected → Falls → Body contacts the platform → Gets up again. High Wall: Running start → Steps on the wall → Inertia upwards → Hands grab the top of the wall → Body hangs → Muscles pull upwards. Soft Hammer: Mechanism activated → Soft hammer sweeps → Hit the body → Body shifts under force → Hands detach from the wall edge → Gravity causes a fall. Waterfall: Descending from the air → Body contacts the water surface → Large splash → Water ripples spread → Character enters the water → Resurfaces. The body cannot anticipate the outcome before the collision occurs. All actions must have a clear cause → Contact → Force → Body feedback → Final result. [Character Performance Curve] The character&#39;s emotions must change naturally with the competition: Opening: Beautiful, confident, relaxed → Rolling: Beginning to feel nervous, but still in control → Fishbone: Noticeably disheveled, flustered, truly falls → Getting up again: Unwilling to give up, continuing to sprint → High wall: Exhausted, giving it their all → Grabbing the top of the wall: Excitement of &quot;finally about to succeed&quot; → Soft hammer appears: Sudden surprise → Falling into the water: Utter failure → Finale: Aggrieved, helpless, with a slight comedic element. The characters&#39; movements are not the perfect action performance of professional athletes. The overall performance maintains the natural physical reactions of an ordinary adult challenger participating in a large-scale entertainment competition program. [Sound Design] The sound of a real large-scale television sports entertainment program is preserved throughout. Includes: the initial buzzing sound, live cheers, applause, screams, laughter, commentary, footsteps of the characters, the sound of the rotating rollers, the sound of the Fishbone rotating, the soft, muffled sound of the soft bar hitting a person, the sound of a person falling off a platform, the sound of a person&#39;s rapid breathing, the sound of someone stepping on a high wall, the sound of the soft hammer mechanism starting, the sound of the soft hammer colliding, the loud sound of a person falling into the water from a height, the splashing sound of water and the surrounding environment. The commentary must change in real time according to the current actions of the characters. When the Fishbone falls, the audience clearly gasps. When the character gets up again, the cheers resume. When the character grabs the top of the high wall, the cheers reach their maximum. When the soft hammer suddenly hits a character, the entire audience gasps instantly. After the character falls into the water, the cheers turn into screams, laughter, and applause. The background music is tense instrumental music with the atmosphere of a large-scale Japanese sports entertainment special. It gradually intensifies as the competition progresses. As a unique architectural element of the city, there are gigantic &quot;record walls&quot; on the building walls that contain a large number of thin, blank record slabs. They are depicted as part of the city&#39;s architecture, not as on-screen UI. The plazas, corridors, elevators, and record walls are filled with the activities of citizens and record officers, with each shot showing 2-3 types of city activities. The main characters are kept to a minimum, making the city itself the main subject of the video. The protagonist is a female record officer in her late twenties. Dark brown bob haircut, intelligent and strong eyes, ivory to light gray long jacket, black long boots, a touch of vermilion. The chief censor is in his late 40s to 50s, tall and large, with short silver-gray hair, a black high-collared long coat, broad shoulders, and only a part of the inside is deep vermilion. Blacking out only appears for the moment the erasure is at work. Immediately after a part of a person or building is briefly covered in black, that part turns into a blank space where nothing is drawn. It is not treated as a simple horizontal line or black bar wipe. Editorial motion graphics cut, cut, compress, overlap, and reconstruct the finished theatrical animation screen, transforming it into a different finished image. [Flow of the video] Starting with a beautiful and gigantic finished image of the record control city, without a 0.0 to 2.5 second wipe. People moving through multi-layered corridors, carts carrying record boxes, large elevators moving up and down in the distance. The camera makes short horizontal movements to convey the scale of the city. After showing the world sufficiently, a portion of the aerial corridor in the distance and a few figures suddenly disappear, leaving a complete void. The void slowly expands, and the scene transitions with a &quot;missing area expansion wipe&quot; revealing the next city section from within. 2.5-5.3 seconds: Another section with a massive vertical record wall and elevators. As the giant elevators rise and the rows of stone slabs on the record wall move mechanically, the disappearances chain together across multiple levels of the city. The upper corridor, a portion of the record wall, and the figures on the lower levels disappear at different times, showing that the anomaly has expanded to a city scale. The vertical frame of the rising elevator divides the screen, and the next completed image fits into the dividing point with a &quot;vertical layer shift wipe&quot; transition. 5.3-7.2 seconds: Here, the protagonist is clearly shown for the first time. In a bustling plaza, the protagonist briefly stops in the middle ground (3/4). A short close-up of the eyes. People move in the background, and a portion of the record wall disappears. No long character scenes take place. The completed image is sharply divided into 2-3 frames along the diagonal lines of the building&#39;s columns, stairs, and windows, each shifting and being reassembled into the next urban composition in a &quot;cut-and-wipe&quot; effect. 7.2-9.3 seconds: High-density MG focusing on the city itself, rather than on the people. Missing corridors, moving elevators, squares where the crowd has thinned out, a massive record wall, and architectural sections that are covered in black and immediately turn into blank spaces are rapidly switched as completed animated screens. The screen is compressed, overlapped, and cut to reconstruct a different urban image. 9.3-10.8 seconds: Inside the Central Archives. The chief censor stands silently behind tall bookshelves and a long staircase. He conveys an imposing presence as a figure in a single 3/4 middle-ground composition. A large black censorship stamp is pressed once. The moment the vermilion stamp&#39;s shadow appears, the entire structure of the screen shifts for a split second and switches to the next scene in a &quot;stamp shift wipe&quot; effect. 10.8-13.5 seconds: The highlight. Erasure and reconstruction proceed simultaneously throughout the entire city. Multilayered corridors, record walls, stairs, elevators, crowds, and architectural walls are divided into different screen layers, some becoming blank, some shifting laterally, some being compressed, and others being reconstructed in different positions. The protagonist appears only once in a short key pose within this gigantic urban transformation. Black paint approaches just before his body, briefly covering only the tip of his jacket hem, and then that part disappears. The protagonist does not run away, but takes just one step forward. At that moment, the entire city separates into multiple completed picture fragments, and after a short inversion and compression, they all fit together into the final composition in a &quot;large-scale reconstruction wipe.&quot; From 13.5 to 15.0 seconds, no wipe is used. The gigantic record city continues to move, albeit partially missing. In the distance, elevators and small human figures are moving slightly. The protagonist does not occupy the center of the city, but stands as a strong point in the screen. A small, erased defect remains at the hem of his jacket. The chief censor is not explicitly shown again, leaving only a faint presence of a human figure high up in the dark, distant Central Archives. The video quietly concludes with the game&#39;s PV key visual, where the city, the missing elements, and the protagonist are united. [Wipe/Editing] The transition method changes in the following order: missing element expansion → vertical layer shift → cutting → stamp shift → large-scale reconstruction. The same wipe is not repeated. Wipes are not used at the beginning and end. Simple black fades, black bars running from side to side, and the same full-screen wipe every time are not used. [Sound] There is background music and sound effects. There are no lines of dialogue or narration. The score is a dark, tense, minimalist drama score mainly consisting of low strings, dry bass percussion, and short metallic percussion. The beat is not mainly electronic synth. The beginning is quiet low strings. The density is gradually increased from 2.5 to 9.3 seconds. The number of sounds is reduced once during the chief censor&#39;s scene, and a heavy, short confirmation sound is made for the censorship stamp. From 10.8 to 13.5 seconds, the strings, percussion, and editing sound effects are increased to maximum density. After 13.5 seconds, it rapidly converges, ending with the lingering resonance of low strings. Missing parts are accompanied by short, sharp erasure sounds, cutting by dry severing sounds, and reconstruction by heavy sounds that sound like multiple structures instantly fitting together. [NEGATIVE] Avoid large amounts of legible text, cards and explanatory UI, backgrounds like graph paper or city planning maps, the entire film consisting only of one or two main characters and an empty background, the use of mannequins for characters, images consisting only of abstract shapes, typical urban destruction with rubble and explosions, neon-dominated cyberpunk, live-action, and realistic 3DCG.

Her arm leads and the mark follows — the timing of the arm and the timing of the mark must match frame for frame. Marks NEVER fade in as a whole word. The arm motion is fluid and human: the wrist flexes, the elbow opens and closes, the shoulder carries the reach across the board, her body weight shifts slightly as she reaches. (0:16–0:17.5) A single sheet of electric blue paper, come down from the top, covers the frame. Its lower edge is torn, with the white core of the paper showing. (0:17.5–0:20) A bright off-white paper field. At the centre, two lines of thin capitals: LAND LIGHT, RUN LONG. No other lettering and no symbol. setting:

  time_of_day: late afternoon / early evening, warm light through windows
  environment_details: rows of desks and chairs as soft silhouettes in the background, chalkboard
